The decisive error was 20. Qe4, which put my queen on a square the knight on f6 attacked. I had won the exchange and was ahead on material before that. I've saved a revised strategy brain (version 2) that adds a rule to check whether my queen or rook can be hit by a minor piece or pawn before each move, and to slow down on the captures after move 15.
